# Somali translation register

Generated by `npm run i18n:register` (web-panel) from the translation catalogue (`web-panel/src/i18n/messages/*.ts`) and the web-panel sources; a test fails if this file is out of date. The panel is shown in English (the default) or Somali on **every** screen, sign-in included; the choice is kept in the browser under `fayadhowr.ui.language`. Stored values (`applicant`, `damiin_needed`, `waiting`, ...), API fields, permission identifiers, audit-log text and the Excel files produced by the backend are never translated; only the visible label differs.

## How to read it

- **Review**: `approved` = owner-approved text (the ID is the owner's review-list ID); `owner-term` = the owner supplied the wording but its review is still open; `proposed` = Somali written for this implementation, not reviewed by a native speaker.
- **Locked**: `locked` marks the 161 messages the owner declared final, approved and locked on 2026-10-27. Their exact text is protected by an automated test against a fixed baseline.
- **Screens**: the source files that use the message (the first six); `not displayed` means no source file refers to it.

## Status of the messages

| Status | Meaning | May it change? |
|---|---|---|
| **locked** (`approved`, 161 messages) | The owner declared these 161 messages FINAL, APPROVED AND LOCKED on 2026-10-27. Exact Somali wording, spelling, capitalization and punctuation, their ids, their English equivalents and their use on screens are protected by `web-panel/src/i18n/lockedApproved.test.ts` against a fixed baseline (`web-panel/src/i18n/baseline/approved-161.json`, SHA-256 `ad500a9affadf2bee600acd67b4827c579bbf7b9a2209e54dde33b5ecdf2cecc`). | **No.** No review or re-approval is requested. |
| **owner-approved addition** (`approved`, 2 messages: `OWN-U1-001`, `OWN-U1-002`) | The two warnings whose Somali the owner supplied and approved under decision U1 (an employee with an active Permanent assignment cannot become Temporary; an employee with an overlapping assignment cannot cover). The same Somali is in the backend; a test keeps the copies identical. Not part of the 161. | No. |
| **owner-term** (23 messages) | The owner supplied the wording; its review is still open. They stay in this category until the owner approves them. | Only by the owner's decision. |
| **proposed** (all others) | Somali written for this implementation from the approved vocabulary. Not reviewed by a native speaker; never to be described as owner-approved. | Yes, after review. |

## Owner decisions applied

- 2026-10-22: Voided is `Laga noqday` (facility status and notices, workday status). The approved buttons `Ka noqo` and `Ka noqo Lacag-bixinta` are unchanged. Finalized payroll cannot be edited **or reopened**. Uploads use `Soo geli`; Excel imports use `Soo geli Excel`, and the confirmation keeps the row count: `Soo geli Excel (safafka diyaarka ah: {n})`. Facility inspection results are `La aqbali karo` / `Lama aqbali karo`. Unchanged on purpose: the Archived variants (`La kaydiyey`, `la dhigay kaydka`, `kaydsan`); Rejected (`Dadka la kansalay`, `La Kansalay`, `Dadka mar hore la kansala`); the three Waiting names (`Shaqa sugayaal`, `Liiska Sugitaanka`, `Falanqaynta Sugitaanka`). The employee status lifecycle is called `wareegga xaaladaha shaqaalaha`. Dates and months follow the language: Somali month names (`Oktoobar 2026`, `1 Okt 2026`) and a 24-hour clock (the Somali month names are proposed wording).
- 2026-10-27: the 161 `approved` messages are final and locked (see above). Somali is shown on **every** screen, sign-in included; English remains the default; the choice is kept in the browser under `fayadhowr.ui.language`.

## Corrections made in this implementation

Five proposed messages described the older payroll rule ("only verified days are charged"). They were corrected to the coverage-period rule (the cost is of the days the cover existed; verified attendance days are only a record). No payroll calculation was changed.

| Key | Correction |
|---|---|
| `confirm.pipeline.approveBody` | text corrected (same key) |
| `warning.pipeline.workdaysNotice` | text corrected (same key); the bold part is now `warning.pipeline.coverageDaysStrong` |
| `warning.payroll.coverSummary.one` / `.other` | "verified day(s)" removed (same keys) |
| `option.pipeline.verifiedDaysCharged` | replaced by `option.pipeline.daysCovered` (old key removed) |
| `warning.pipeline.onlyVerifiedCharged` | replaced by `warning.pipeline.coverageDaysStrong` (old key removed) |

## Open points (none concerns the locked messages)

- Every `proposed` message is a draft written for this implementation. **None has been reviewed by a native Somali speaker**, including warnings and confirmations.
- Waiting vs Waiting List: the sidebar and the status use the owner wording `Shaqa sugayaal`, the move-to-stage choice uses the approved `Liiska Sugitaanka`; the shared phrase was left as the owner approved it.
- `Employee Registration` and `Employees` open the same page; the menu structure was not changed.
- `Facility Report` (HR-NAV-038) was never on the owner's review list; its Somali (`Warbixinta Xarumaha`) is proposed.
- The owner text for `Advance to {Status}` wrote the variable as `{Xaalad}`; the variable name is kept as `{status}`.
- `Void` / `Void day` / `Void payment` share the approved `Ka noqo`; the three actions have different consequences, so their explanations differ.
- Terminology variants among the **proposed** messages (the same English with different Somali, for example Active, Absent, Company, Cancelled, Completed) are documented in `PREVIOUS_SOMALI_TRANSLATIONS_INVENTORY_2026-10-27.md`; a glossary is recommended. Variants that involve a locked message keep the locked wording exactly.
- Left in English on purpose: proper names and technical terms (Excel, CSV, PDF, USD, SMTP, file extensions, identifiers), XARUN, PROJECT and Damiin (the owner's own terms), and text that comes from the server or from users (names, notes, audit-log descriptions, and API messages for which no mapped error code exists). The Excel files the backend produces keep their English column headings.
- Server messages (added after the language layer): Laravel's standard validation sentences ("The full name field is required.", length, number, date, file, password and "selected is invalid" rules), the sign-in answers (`Invalid email or password.`, inactive account, login failed, too many attempts) and the permission answers are shown in Somali by matching the sentence the server sends against the catalogue (`validation.laravel.*`, `notify.server.*`, field names in `validation.field.*`). English is never rewritten. A sentence that matches nothing keeps the server's own text. All of these are `proposed`.
- Still shown as the server wrote them in Somali mode: the app's own multi-part validation sentences that contain names, lists or dates (facility inspection and complaint rules, assignment rules, settings rules), field names without a `validation.field.*` entry (the English field name is kept inside the Somali sentence), nested field names such as `complaints.0.details`, and the browser's own pop-ups for `required` / `type=email` inputs (they follow the browser's language, not the panel's).
- A dialog that has already shown a validation error keeps that text in the language it was shown in until the form is submitted again; the sign-in page re-translates its message immediately when the language is switched.

| `warning.payroll.correctionsExplain` |  |  | The closed month is never changed. An overpayment is taken from the employee's later salaries (never more than that month's pay, and from the final salary when they leave); what is left stays a debt that a Super Admin or an HR Manager manages here. A Super Admin can write off what cannot be recovered (with a reason; the debt and its history stay). An underpayment is owed to the employee and is paid only by a Super Admin, as a separate recorded payment, which only a Super Admin can void (a linked reversal is added; nothing is deleted). | Bisha xidhan weligeed lama beddelo. Lacagta dheeraadka ah waxaa laga qaadaa mushaharyada dambe ee shaqaalaha (weligeed ma dhaafto mushaharka bishaas, waxaana laga qaadaa mushaharka ugu dambeeya marka uu shaqada ka tago); wixii hadha waxay noqonayaan deyn halkan ka maamula Super Admin ama HR Manager. Super Admin wuxuu ka cafi karaa waxa aan la soo celin karin (sabab la siiyo; deynta iyo taariikhdeeda way hadhayaan). Lacagta ka yaraaday waa lagu leeyahay shaqaalaha, waxaana bixin kara Super Admin keliya, isagoo u bixinaya lacag-bixin gaar ah oo la diiwaangeliyey, taas oo Super Admin keliya uu ka noqon karo (rogid ku xiran ayaa lagu daraa; waxba lama tirtiro). | proposed | PayrollCorrectionsPanel.tsx |  |
| `warning.payroll.voidsExplain` |  |  | A payment marked paid was cancelled by a Super Admin or an HR Manager. The original payment stays in the payment ledger; a linked reversal of the same amount was added, and the payroll line can be paid again. | Lacag-bixin loo calaamadeeyey inay la bixiyey waxaa joojiyey Super Admin ama HR Manager. Lacag-bixinta asalka ah waxay ku hadhaysaa diiwaanka lacag-bixinta; rogid ku xiran oo isla qaddarkaas ah ayaa lagu daray, safka mushaharkana waa la bixin karaa mar kale. | proposed | PayrollCorrectionsPanel.tsx |  |
